What this repo does: Fort is a single-owner native messenger with a Node core and
optional official runtime adapters. This review covers publication of the preserved
branch history and the exact local launch instructions, assuming one owner.

Reviewed the complete changed-path inventory, the existing whole-tree baseline,
current acceptance ledger and final semantic slice reports in Git history. Fresh
inspection focuses on what will become public: all new blob versions and archives,
evidence-image text, provenance/legal notices, quarantine boundaries, workflow
permissions and actual UI labels. The 261-commit range includes a quarantined donor
and historical prototypes; it is not a single new product implementation slice.

Must fix (resolved): the prior current report covered only the final evidence
commit. A complete-range CI check would reject it. This record now binds the whole
resulting range while retaining the earlier semantic reviews and their limits.

No live credential or scratch credential artifact was identified in the publication
audit. The untracked owner approval and build scratch stay outside publication.
The remote is already public; no visibility change is proposed. No hooks, startup
services, sign-in, provider prompts or deployment are part of this review.

Verdict: ready for the authorized fast-forward publication. Remote SHA/CI success
must be checked after push. This is not full v1 or TestFlight signoff.
Not checked: a fresh line-by-line audit of all historical code, new full regressions,
new-source provider behavior, human IME use, physical iPhone, TestFlight, effective
model attestation or provider grant closure. Prior temporary artifacts are absent.
